def obj_var(lam):
    h=G@lam; return ((h-h.mean())**2).sum()
def grad_var(lam):
    h=G@lam; p=h-h.mean(); return G.T@p
res=minimize(obj_var, lam0, jac=grad_var, method='SLSQP', bounds=[(0,1)]*K, constraints={'type':'eq','fun':lambda...})

Implementation TopSLE:
```
class _TopSLE:
    def __init__(self,beta,K,n): self.beta=beta; self.K=K; self.n=n
    def _tops(self,c):
        K=self.K
        m=len(c)
        if K>=m: return np.arange(m)
        # use argpartition for speed
        ind=np.argpartition(c, -K)[-K:]
        return ind
    def _vals_weights(self,c):
        m=len(c)
        cmax=float(c.max())
        if self.K>=m:
           idx=np.arange(m); vals=c
        else:
           idx=np.argpartition(c, -self.K)[-self.K:]
           vals=c[idx]
        vmax=float(vals.max())  # should equal cmax
        sh=(vals-vmax)/self.beta
        e=np.exp(np.clip(sh,-700,0))
        s=e.sum()
        if s<1e-300 or not np.isfinite(s):
           w=np.zeros_like(vals); w[int(np.argmax(vals))]=1.0
        else:
           w=e/s
        # value: if K==1 return cmax; else cmax + beta*log(s/K)
        val = cmax + self.beta*np.log(s/ max(1,self.K))
        return val, idx, w
    def __call__(self,z):
        zc = z - np.mean(z)
        a = _softmax(zc)
        c = _fft_convolve(a,a)
        val, idx, w = self._vals_weights(c)
        return float(2.0*self.n*val)
    def grad(self,z):
        zc=z-np.mean(z)
        a=_softmax(zc)
        c=_fft_convolve(a,a)
        val, idx, w = self._vals_weights(c)
        # W = dot(w, vals) where vals=c[idx]
        W=float(np.dot(w, c[idx]))
        wv=np.zeros(len(c)); wv[idx]=w
        ar=a[::-1]
        h=_fft_convolve(wv, ar)[:2*self.n-1]  # conv length (2n-1)+(n)-1=3n-2? Wait wv length 2n-1, ar length n -> length 3n-2. We need h length n centered: _fft_convolve returns full length 3n-2. Need extract [n-1:2n-1]
        h = _fft_convolve(wv, ar)[self.n-1:2*self.n-1]
        grad = 4.0*self.n*a*(h - W)
        return grad
```
Check convolve lengths: `_fft_convolve(a,a)` with lengths n,n returns 2n-1. For `_fft_convolve(wv, ar)` lengths 2n-1,n returns 3n-2. We need h_i=∑_j a_j w_{i+j}. Using formula conv(w,a_rev)[n-1:2n-1]. Yes. But if we compute full length 3n-2, slice n-1 to 2n-1 inclusive? Python [n-1:2n-1] length n. Good.

Weighted polish function details:
```
def _weighted_polish(a_init, deadline, min_time_remaining=20.0, max_K=64):
    n=len(a); a=a_init.copy(); a/=sum
    z=np.log(a); z-=z.mean()
    best=a.copy(); best_sc=_score(a)
    K=16; outer=0; no_improve=0
    while time+min_time<deadline:
       a=_softmax(z); c=conv; sc=2n*c.max(); update best
       # active top K: maybe start 16, increase to 64
       # Build G
       G = _build_G(c, a, K)
       # Solve lambda
       lam = _solve_lambda(a, G, c[idx])
       h=G@lam; W=float(np.dot(lam,c[idx])); p=h-W
       zdir = a*p
       norm=norm(zdir)
       if norm < 1e-18: break
       # scale zdir: max_abs = max(abs(zdir))/norm? To make max step 1
       scale = max(np.max(np.abs(zdir)), 1e-300)
       zdir = zdir / scale
       # line search over eta in log? Start 0.5
       eta=0.5
       improved=False
       current_sc=sc
       for it in range(30):
           z_try = z - eta*zdir
           a_try=_softmax(z_try)
           sc_try=_score(a_try)
           if sc_try < current_sc - 1e-14:
              z=z_try; a=a_try; improved=True; break
           eta*=0.5
       if improved: no_improve=0; K=min(max_K, K+1) maybe grow
       else:
          no_improve+=1
          if no_improve %10==0: K=max(8,K//2)
          if no_improve>=100: z=np.log(best); z-=z.mean(); K=16; no_improve=0; if no_improve>200 break
       # Occasionally recenter z to avoid drift
       z-=z.mean()
    return best,best_sc
```
But `_solve_lambda` each outer expensive. We can reduce outer by time. K start 8 or 16. Use λ solve 50 iters. Good.

Build G:
```
def _build_G(a, idx, K):
    n=len(a); G=np.empty((K,n))
    for r,k in enumerate(idx):
       row=np.zeros(n)
       start=max(0,k-n+1); end=min(n,k+1)
       if end>start:
          row[start:end]=a[k-start : k-end+1 : -1]
       G[r]=row
    return G
```
Check slicing: Example n=5,k=3. start=0,end=4. k-start=3, k-end+1=0, slice 3:0:-1 -> indices 3,2,1,0 assigned to row 0..3: row0=a3,row1=a2,row2=a1,row3=a0. Formula g_i=a_{k-i}: i=0 a3,1 a2,2 a1,3 a0. Good.
If k=6,n=5. start=2,end=5. slice 6-2=4 to 6-5+1=2 step -1 -> 4,3,2 assigned row2..4: g2=a4,g3=a3,g4=a2. Correct.

`_solve_lambda(a,G,c_active)`:
```
def _solve_lambda(a,G,c_active,max_iter=80):
    K=len(c_active)
    lam=np.ones(K)/K
    a2=a*a
    val=None
    for it in range(max_iter):
       h=G@lam
       W=float(np.dot(lam,c_active))
       p=h-W
       val=float(np.dot(a2,p*p))
       ap=a2*p
       gp=G.T@ap
       S=float(ap.sum())
       grad=2.0*(gp - S*c_active)
       # gradient step projected
       step=1.0 / max(1e-12, np.linalg.norm(grad, ord=np.inf))
       accepted=False
       for _ in range(20):
           lam_new=_proj_simplex(lam - step*grad)
           h2=G@lam_new
           W2=float(np.dot(lam_new,c_active))
           p2=h2-W2
           val2=float(np.dot(a2,p2*p2))
           if val2 < val - 1e-30:
              lam=lam_new; val=val2; accepted=True; break
           step*=0.5
       if not accepted: break
    return lam
```

```
class _TopSLEFixed:
  def __init__(self,beta,idx,n): ...
  def fgrad(z):
     a=softmax; c=conv; vals=c[idx]; vmax=vals.max(); sh=...
     val = vmax + beta*np.log(s/len(idx))
     W=dot(w,vals)
     wv zeros; wv[idx]=w
     h=conv(wv,a_rev)[n-1:2n-1]
     grad=4n a*(h-W)
     return 2n*val, grad
```

Now, proof of `_score`: For a list or array normalized sum1. Use `_fft_convolve` full conv. If a is length n, b length n; returns 2n-1. Score = 2.0*n*float(c.max())/s*s? If a normalized, s~1. But for general, use s. `_score`:
```
def _score(a):
    a=np.asarray(a,dtype=float)
    s=a.sum()
    if s<=0: return np.inf
    c=_fft_convolve(a,a)
    return float(2.0*len(a)*float(c.max())/(s*s))
```
For normalized, okay.

`_softmax`:
```
def _softmax(z):
    z = np.asarray(z, dtype=float)
    z = z - np.max(z)
    z = np.clip(z, -700, 700)
    e=np.exp(z)
    s=e.sum()
    if s<=0 or not finite: return np.ones_like(z)/len(z)
    return e/s
```
Using max not mean; if z all very negative? max removes.

`_log_from_a(a)`:
```
a=np.maximum(a,0); s=a.sum(); if s<... return zeros; a=a/s; z=np.log(np.maximum(a,1e-300)); return z-z.mean()
```

`_resample`:
```
def _resample(a,new_n):
    a=np.asarray(a,dtype=float)
    old_n=len(a)
    if new_n<=0: return ...
    if old_n==new_n: return a.copy()
    x_old=np.linspace(0,1,old_n)
    x_new=np.linspace(0,1,new_n)
    a_new=np.interp(x_new,x_old,a)
    a_new=np.maximum(a_new,0)
    s=a_new.sum()
    if s>0: a_new/=s
    return a_new
```

`_top_indices(c,K)`:
```
def _top_indices(c,K):
    m=len(c)
    K=min(K,m)
    if K>=m: return np.arange(m)
    return np.argpartition(c, -K)[-K:]
```

TopSLE fixed idx class:
```
class _TopSLE:
    def __init__(self,beta,idx,n):
        self.beta=float(beta); self.idx=np.asarray(idx,dtype=int); self.n=int(n); self.m=self.idx.size
    def fgrad(self,z):
        zc = z - np.mean(z)
        a = _softmax(zc)
        c = _fft_convolve(a,a)
        vals = c[self.idx]
        vmax = float(np.max(vals))
        sh = (vals - vmax) / self.beta
        e = np.exp(np.clip(sh, -700, 0.0))
        s = float(e.sum())
        if s < 1e-300 or not np.isfinite(s):
            w = np.zeros(self.m)
            w[int(np.argmax(vals))] = 1.0
        else:
            w = e / s
        val = vmax + self.beta * np.log(s / self.m)
        W = float(np.dot(w, vals))
        wv = np.zeros(2*self.n-1)
        wv[self.idx] = w
        ar = a[::-1]
        tmp = _fft_convolve(wv, ar)
        h = tmp[self.n-1:2*self.n-1]
        grad = 4.0 * self.n * a * (h - W)
        return float(2.0 * self.n * val), grad
```

Pseudo:
```
def _weighted_polish(a_init, deadline, min_time_remaining, K_max=64):
    n=len(a_init)
    a=_normalize(a_init)
    z=_log_from_a(a)
    best=a.copy(); best_sc=_score(a)
    K=8; no=0
    while time+min_time<deadline:
       a=_softmax(z); c=_fft_convolve(a,a); sc=2n*c.max()
       if sc<best_sc: best=a.copy(); best_sc=sc
       idx=_top_indices(c,K)
       G=_build_G(a, idx)
       lam=_solve_lambda(a,G,c[idx],max_iter=50)
       h=G@lam; W=float(np.dot(lam,c[idx])); p=h-W
       zdir=a*p
       znorm=float(np.linalg.norm(zdir))
       if znorm < 1e-18:
           K=min(K_max,K*2) if K<K_max else break
           continue
       scale=max(float(np.max(np.abs(zdir))),1e-300)
       zdir=zdir/scale
       eta=0.7
       improved=False
       for _ in range(25):
           z_try=z - eta*zdir
           a_try=_softmax(z_try)
           sc_try=_score(a_try)
           if sc_try < sc - 1e-14:
               z=z_try; a=a_try; improved=True; break
           eta*=0.5
       if improved:
           no=0
           if K < K_max and no % 5 ==0: K=min(K_max,K+1) # too fast? use every 10
       else:
           no+=1
           if no==10: K=max(8,K//2)
           if no>=80:
              z=_log_from_a(best); K=8; no=0
              if no >= 160: break
       z = z - np.mean(z)
    return best,best_sc
```
Grow K every 20 improvements: `if improved and (iter_count % 10 ==0): K=min(K_max,K+1)`. Need iter_count.

`_proj_simplex`: standard. Need handle v length K; if K=1 returns [1]. Implementation:
```
def _proj_simplex(v):
    n=len(v)
    if n==1: return np.array([1.0])
    u=np.sort(v)[::-1]
    cssv=np.cumsum(u)
    rho=np.arange(1,n+1)
    cond = u * rho > (cssv - 1.0)
    if not np.any(cond): return np.ones(n)/n
    rho=int(np.nonzero(cond)[0][-1])
    theta=(cssv[rho]-1.0)/(rho+1.0)
    return np.maximum(v-theta,0.0)
```
Works.

`_run_top_lbfgs` code with phases:
```
def _run_top_lbfgs(a_init, deadline, min_time_remaining, max_K=512):
    n=len(a_init)
    a=_normalize(a_init)
    best=a.copy(); best_sc=_score(a)
    z=_log_from_a(a)
    max_K=min(max_K,2*n-1)
    c=_fft_convolve(a,a); cmax=float(c.max())
    c_sorted=np.sort(c)[::-1]
    Kref=min(128,len(c))
    gap=c_sorted[0]-c_sorted[Kref-1]
    base_beta=max(gap*1.5, cmax*1e-4, 1e-12)
    min_beta=max(cmax*1e-7, 1e-14)
    factors=np.array([...])
    K_list=[8,16,32,64,128,128,256,256,256,512,512,512,512]
    # adjust max
    phase_end = deadline - float(min_time_remaining)
    # if phase_end <= time+5: return
    for i in range(len(factors)):
       if time.monotonic() >= phase_end - 5: break
       K=int(min(max_K, K_list[min(i,len(K_list)-1)]))
       beta=max(base_beta*factors[i], min_beta)
       a_cur=_softmax(z); idx=_top_indices(_fft_convolve(a_cur,a_cur),K)
       obj=_TopSLE(beta,idx,n)
       remaining=phase_end - time.monotonic()
       # allocate at most min(remaining, 120?)? We want spread. Use remaining/(len(factors)-i) *0.9
       run_budget = remaining / (len(factors)-i) * 0.85
       if run_budget < 5: run_budget=5
       est_eval = 0.005 * max(1.0, n/4096.0)
       maxfun=int(min(50000, max(20, run_budget/est_eval*0.7)))
       maxiter=int(min(20000, max(20, maxfun//5+10)))
       old_z=z.copy()
       try:
          res=minimize(obj.fgrad, z, jac=True, method='L-BFGS-B', options={...})
          if res.success or res.status in (0,1,2,4,5,6?) maybe just if x finite:
             new_z=np.asarray(res.x)
             diff=new_z-old_z
             # clip diff to 2
             diff=np.clip(diff,-2.0,2.0)
             cand_z=old_z + diff
             cand_z-=np.mean(cand_z)
             cand_a=_softmax(cand_z)
             cand_sc=_score(cand_a)
             if cand_sc < best_sc - 1e-15:
                 best=cand_a.copy(); best_sc=cand_sc
             # update z for iteration: if cand_sc <= best_sc + 1e-4*best_sc or cand_sc < old_score? We don't have old score. Use if cand_sc < best_sc + 1e-5:
             if np.all(np.isfinite(cand_z)):
                 z=cand_z
       except Exception:
          pass
    return best,best_sc
```

`_run_reduced_lbfgs`: Similar with outer. We can set max_outer=15, each run budget allocated. K maybe 64. Q basis building. Use beta small: `beta=max(cmax*1e-5,1e-12)`. For reduced objective, K maybe 64; L-BFGS dims. maxfun based on time. Outer loop:
```
def _run_reduced_lbfgs(a_init, deadline, min_time_remaining, K=64):
  n; a normalize; z; best
  phase_end=deadline-min_time
  max_outer=12
  for outer in range(max_outer):
    if time>=phase_end-10 break
    a=softmax(z); c=conv; sc=score; update
    idx=topK
    D=_build_directions(a, idx, c)
    # QR
    try Q,R=np.linalg.qr(D.T, mode='reduced') except: break
    r=Q.shape[1]
    if r<1: break
    # drop small: diagR=np.abs(np.diag(R)); r_kept=sum>1e-8
    if r_kept>0: Q=Q[:, :r_kept]; r=r_kept
    else: break
    beta=max(cmax*1e-5,1e-12)
    obj=_ReducedSLE(z,Q,idx,beta,n)
    remaining=phase_end-time
    run_budget=remaining/(max_outer-outer)*0.8
    est_eval=0.006*max(1,n/4096)
    maxfun=int(min(30000,max(50,run_budget/est_eval*0.6)))
    x0=np.zeros(r)
    bounds=[(-3.0,3.0)]*r
    try res=minimize(obj.fgrad,x0,jac=True,method='L-BFGS-B',bounds=bounds,options={...})
    if x finite:
       z_new=z+Q@alpha; clip diff; a_new; sc_new; update; if sc_new<sc+... update z
  return best,best_sc
```
`_build_directions` returns D (K x n). Need c idx values. We can build using G:
```
G=_build_G(a, idx)
D=np.empty_like(G)
for r,k in enumerate(idx):
   d=a*(G[r]-c[k])
   norm=np.linalg.norm(d)
   D[r]=d/norm if norm>1e-15 else 0
return D
```
Memory D similar to G. Could avoid storing both? Just D.

Orthogonalizing D.T (n x K). If K=64, QR fine. But D rows may have large norm? normalized. Good.

Backtracking in `_solve_lambda` may still be slow. Let's implement with a small fixed number:
```
def _solve_lambda(a,G,c_active,max_iter=30):
    K=len(c_active); lam=np.ones(K)/K
    a2=a*a*float(n*n) # n from a
    for it in range(max_iter):
        h=G@lam; W=float(np.dot(lam,c_active)); p=h-W
        ap=a2*p
        gp=G.T@ap; S=float(ap.sum()); grad=2.0*(gp - S*c_active)
        gnorm=float(np.linalg.norm(grad, ord=np.inf))
        if gnorm<1e-30: break
        step=1.0/gnorm
        # try step, 0.5,0.25
        accepted=False
        for st in (step,0.5*step,0.25*step,0.125*step):
            lam_new=_proj_simplex(lam - st*grad)
            if np.linalg.norm(lam_new-lam)<1e-16: break?
            # compute val
            h2=G@lam_new; W2=dot(lam_new,c_active); p2=h2-W2
            val2=float(np.dot(a2,p2*p2))
            # need current val
```

```python
"""
Local-first optimizer for

    2*n*max(conv(a,a)) / (sum(a)**2)

with a_i >= 0.  The strategy starts from the supplied parent sequence, tries a
few cheap resamplings and asymmetric multiplicative perturbations, and then
performs several phases of smoothing active peaks:

1. Fixed top-K log-sum-exp L-BFGS in log-space.
2. Reduced-basis L-BFGS using active-peak gradient directions.
3. A custom active-set gradient polish with simplex-weighted peaks.
4. A short multi-length exploration.
5. A final ultra-fine polish.

All helper functions are top-level.  No lambdas or nested closures are used.
"""

import numpy as np
import time
from scipy.optimize import minimize


def _next_pow2(n):
    k = 1
    while k < n:
        k <<= 1
    return max(2, k)


def _fft_convolve(u, v):
    u = np.asarray(u, dtype=np.float64)
    v = np.asarray(v, dtype=np.float64)
    out_n = u.size + v.size - 1
    N = _next_pow2(out_n)
    fu = np.fft.rfft(u, N)
    fv = np.fft.rfft(v, N)
    out = np.fft.irfft(fu * fv, N)
    return out[:out_n]


def _normalize(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s < 1e-300:
        return np.ones_like(a, dtype=np.float64) / max(1, a.size)
    return a / s


def _score(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    if a.size == 0:
        return np.inf
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s < 1e-300:
        return np.inf
    c = _fft_convolve(a, a)
    return float(2.0 * a.size * float(c.max()) / (s * s))


def _softmax(z):
    z = np.asarray(z, dtype=np.float64)
    z = z - np.max(z)
    z = np.clip(z, -700.0, 700.0)
    e = np.exp(z)
    s = float(e.sum())
    if s < 1e-300 or not np.isfinite(s):
        return np.ones_like(z, dtype=np.float64) / max(1, z.size)
    return e / s


def _log_from_a(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s < 1e-300:
        return np.zeros_like(a, dtype=np.float64)
    a = a / s
    z = np.log(np.maximum(a, 1e-300))
    z = z - float(np.mean(z))
    return z


def _top_indices(c, K):
    m = c.size
    K = int(min(K, m))
    if K <= 0:
        return np.empty(0, dtype=np.int64)
    if K >= m:
        return np.arange(m, dtype=np.int64)
    return np.argpartition(c, -K)[-K:]


def _resample(a, new_n):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    new_n = int(new_n)
    if new_n <= 0:
        return a.copy()
    old_n = int(a.size)
    if old_n == new_n:
        return a.copy()
    x_old = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, old_n)
    x_new = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, new_n)
    b = np.interp(x_new, x_old, a)
    b = np.maximum(b, 0.0)
    s = float(b.sum())
    if s > 1e-300:
        b /= s
    else:
        b = np.ones(new_n, dtype=np.float64) / new_n
    return b


def _proj_simplex(v):
    v = np.asarray(v, dtype=np.float64)
    n = v.size
    if n == 1:
        return np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)
    u = np.sort(v)[::-1]
    cssv = np.cumsum(u)
    rho = np.arange(1, n + 1, dtype=np.float64)
    cond = u * rho > (cssv - 1.0 + 1e-18)
    if not np.any(cond):
        return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / n
    idx = int(np.nonzero(cond)[0][-1])
    theta = (cssv[idx] - 1.0) / (idx + 1.0)
    return np.maximum(v - theta, 0.0)


class _TopSLE:
    """
    Fixed-index log-sum-exp approximation to the maximum over a subset of
    convolution coefficients.  The returned Jacobian is the correct softmax
    log-space gradient.
    """

    def __init__(self, beta, idx, n):
        self.beta = float(beta)
        self.idx = np.asarray(idx, dtype=np.int64)
        self.n = int(n)
        self.m = int(self.idx.size)

    def fgrad(self, z):
        z = np.asarray(z, dtype=np.float64)
        z = z - float(np.mean(z))
        a = _softmax(z)
        c = _fft_convolve(a, a)
        vals = c[self.idx]
        vmax = float(np.max(vals))

        sh = (vals - vmax) / self.beta
        e = np.exp(np.clip(sh, -700.0, 0.0))
        s = float(e.sum())

        if s < 1e-300 or not np.isfinite(s):
            w = np.zeros(self.m, dtype=np.float64)
            w[int(np.argmax(vals))] = 1.0
        else:
            w = e / s

        val = vmax + self.beta * float(np.log(s / self.m))
        W = float(np.dot(w, vals))

        wv = np.zeros(2 * self.n - 1, dtype=np.float64)
        wv[self.idx] = w
        ar = a[::-1]
        tmp = _fft_convolve(wv, ar)
        h = tmp[self.n - 1:2 * self.n - 1]

        grad = 4.0 * self.n * a * (h - W)
        return float(2.0 * self.n * val), grad


class _ReducedSLE:
    """
    Top-K log-sum-exp SLE objective restricted to a low-dimensional affine
    subspace z = z0 + Q @ alpha.
    """

    def __init__(self, z0, Q, idx, beta, n):
        self.z0 = np.asarray(z0, dtype=np.float64)
        self.Q = np.asarray(Q, dtype=np.float64)
        self.idx = np.asarray(idx, dtype=np.int64)
        self.beta = float(beta)
        self.n = int(n)
        self.m = int(self.idx.size)

    def fgrad(self, alpha):
        alpha = np.asarray(alpha, dtype=np.float64)
        z = self.z0 + self.Q.dot(alpha)
        z = z - float(np.mean(z))
        a = _softmax(z)
        c = _fft_convolve(a, a)
        vals = c[self.idx]
        vmax = float(np.max(vals))

        sh = (vals - vmax) / self.beta
        e = np.exp(np.clip(sh, -700.0, 0.0))
        s = float(e.sum())

        if s < 1e-300 or not np.isfinite(s):
            w = np.zeros(self.m, dtype=np.float64)
            w[int(np.argmax(vals))] = 1.0
        else:
            w = e / s

        val = vmax + self.beta * float(np.log(s / self.m))
        W = float(np.dot(w, vals))

        wv = np.zeros(2 * self.n - 1, dtype=np.float64)
        wv[self.idx] = w
        ar = a[::-1]
        tmp = _fft_convolve(wv, ar)
        h = tmp[self.n - 1:2 * self.n - 1]

        grad_z = 4.0 * self.n * a * (h - W)
        grad_alpha = self.Q.T.dot(grad_z)
        return float(2.0 * self.n * val), grad_alpha


def _build_G(a, idx):
    """
    Build matrix G with rows G[k, i] = a[k-i] when valid, else 0.
    """
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n = a.size
    K = idx.size
    G = np.zeros((K, n), dtype=np.float64)
    if K == 0:
        return G
    for r, k in enumerate(idx):
        k = int(k)
        start = max(0, k - n + 1)
        end = min(n, k + 1)
        if end > start:
            lo = k - end + 1
            hi = k - start + 1
            G[r, start:end] = a[lo:hi][::-1]
    return G


def _build_directions(a, idx, c):
    """
    Active-peak log-space directions: for coefficient k, the log-space gradient
    of c_k is proportional to a_i * (a_{k-i} - c_k).
    """
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n = a.size
    K = idx.size
    D = np.zeros((K, n), dtype=np.float64)
    if K == 0:
        return D
    G = _build_G(a, idx)
    for r, k in enumerate(idx):
        d = a * (G[r] - float(c[int(k)]))
        norm = float(np.linalg.norm(d))
        if norm > 1e-15:
            D[r] = d / norm
    return D


def _solve_lambda(a, G, c_active, max_iter=25):
    """
    Solve, approximately, a projected-gradient simplex problem:

        min_{lambda >= 0, sum lambda = 1}
            || a_i * (G lambda)_i - (lambda^T c_active) ||^2

    This chooses active-peak weights whose weighted gradient is as close as
    possible to a constant vector, the natural first-order KKT condition for
    a local max-peak minimum.
    """
    K = c_active.size
    if K <= 1:
        return np.ones(K, dtype=np.float64)

    lam = np.ones(K, dtype=np.float64) / K
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n = a.size
    scale = float(n) * float(n)
    a2 = (a * a) * scale

    for _ in range(int(max_iter)):
        h = G.dot(lam)
        W = float(np.dot(lam, c_active))
        p = h - W
        ap = a2 * p
        gp = G.T.dot(ap)
        S = float(ap.sum())
        grad = 2.0 * (gp - S * np.asarray(c_active, dtype=np.float64))

        gnorm = float(np.linalg.norm(grad, ord=np.inf))
        if gnorm < 1e-30:
            break
        step = 1.0 / gnorm
        lam = _proj_simplex(lam - step * grad)

    return lam


def _run_top_lbfgs(a_init, deadline, min_time_remaining, max_K=512):
    """
    Main full-dimension L-BFGS using fixed top-K log-sum-exp surrogates.
    """
    a_init = _normalize(a_init)
    n = int(a_init.size)
    a = a_init.copy()
    best_a = a.copy()
    best_sc = _score(a)
    cur_z = _log_from_a(a)

    phase_end = float(deadline) - float(min_time_remaining)
    if time.monotonic() >= phase_end - 5.0:
        return best_a, best_sc

    c0 = _fft_convolve(a, a)
    cmax0 = float(c0.max())
    if cmax0 <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(cmax0):
        return best_a, best_sc

    c_sorted = np.sort(c0)[::-1]
    Kref = min(128, c_sorted.size)
    gap = float(c_sorted[0] - c_sorted[Kref - 1])
    base_beta = max(gap * 1.5, cmax0 * 1e-4, 1e-12)
    min_beta = max(cmax0 * 1e-7, 1e-14)

    factors = np.array([
        1.0, 0.5, 0.25, 0.125, 0.0625, 0.03125, 0.015625,
        0.0078125, 0.0039, 0.0019, 0.0009, 0.0004, 0.0002
    ])
    K_list = [8, 16, 32, 64, 128, 128, 256, 256, 256, 512, 512, 512, 512]
    max_K = int(min(max_K, 2 * n - 1))

    est_eval = 0.005 * max(1.0, n / 4096.0)
    nbeta = len(factors)

    for i, fac in enumerate(factors):
        if time.monotonic() >= phase_end - 5.0:
            break

        K = int(min(max_K, K_list[min(i, len(K_list) - 1)]))
        beta = float(max(base_beta * float(fac), min_beta))

        a_cur = _softmax(cur_z)
        c_cur = _fft_convolve(a_cur, a_cur)
        cur_sc = float(2.0 * n * c_cur.max())
        if cur_sc < best_sc:
            best_sc = cur_sc
            best_a = a_cur.copy()

        idx = _top_indices(c_cur, K)
        if idx.size == 0:
            continue

        obj = _TopSLE(beta, idx, n)
        remaining = float(phase_end - time.monotonic())
        run_budget = remaining / float(max(1, nbeta - i)) * 0.82
        if run_budget < 4.0:
            run_budget = 4.0

        maxfun = int(min(50000, max(20, run_budget / est_eval * 0.72)))
        maxiter = int(min(20000, max(20, maxfun // 5 + 10)))

        old_z = cur_z.copy()
        try:
            res = minimize(
                obj.fgrad,
                cur_z,
                jac=True,
                method='L-BFGS-B',
                options={
                    'maxiter': maxiter,
                    'maxfun': maxfun,
                    'ftol': 1e-18,
                    'gtol': 1e-15,
                    'maxcor': 50,
                    'maxls': 30
                }
            )
            new_z = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64)
            if new_z.size == old_z.size and np.all(np.isfinite(new_z)):
                diff = new_z - old_z
                diff = np.clip(diff, -2.0, 2.0)
                cand_z = old_z + diff
                cand_z = cand_z - float(np.mean(cand_z))
                cand_a = _softmax(cand_z)
                cand_sc = _score(cand_a)

                if cand_sc < best_sc:
                    best_a = cand_a.copy()
                    best_sc = cand_sc

                if cand_sc < cur_sc + 1e-3 * max(1e-300, abs(cur_sc)):
                    cur_z = cand_z
        except Exception:
            pass

    # If the current z-track is better than best_a, use it.
    try:
        a_z = _softmax(cur_z)
        sc_z = _score(a_z)
        if sc_z < best_sc:
            best_a = a_z.copy()
            best_sc = sc_z
    except Exception:
        pass

    return best_a, best_sc


def _run_reduced_lbfgs(a_init, deadline, min_time_remaining, K=48):
    """
    Reduced-basis L-BFGS.  Each outer iteration builds an orthonormal basis from
    active-peak log-space gradient directions, then optimizes only the basis
    coefficients.
    """
    a_init = _normalize(a_init)
    n = int(a_init.size)
    a = a_init.copy()
    best_a = a.copy()
    best_sc = _score(a)
    z0 = _log_from_a(a)

    phase_end = float(deadline) - float(min_time_remaining)
    if time.monotonic() >= phase_end - 10.0:
        return best_a, best_sc

    max_outer = 10
    est_eval = 0.006 * max(1.0, n / 4096.0)

    for outer in range(max_outer):
        if time.monotonic() >= phase_end - 10.0:
            break

        a = _softmax(z0)
        c = _fft_convolve(a, a)
        sc = float(2.0 * n * c.max())
        if sc < best_sc:
            best_sc = sc
            best_a = a.copy()

        idx = _top_indices(c, K)
        if idx.size == 0:
            break

        D = _build_directions(a, idx, c)
        if D.size == 0 or not np.any(np.abs(D) > 1e-15):
            break

        try:
            Q, R = np.linalg.qr(D.T, mode='reduced')
        except Exception:
            break

        if Q.size == 0 or Q.shape[1] == 0:
            break

        diagR = np.abs(np.diag(R))
        keep = int(np.sum(diagR > 1e-8))
        if keep < 1:
            break
        if keep < Q.shape[1]:
            Q = Q[:, :keep]

        r = Q.shape[1]
        beta = max(float(c.max()) * 1e-5, 1e-12)
        obj = _ReducedSLE(z0, Q, idx, beta, n)

        remaining = float(phase_end - time.monotonic())
        run_budget = remaining / float(max(1, max_outer - outer)) * 0.82
        if run_budget < 5.0:
            run_budget = 5.0

        maxfun = int(min(30000, max(20, run_budget / est_eval * 0.65)))
        maxiter = int(min(20000, max(20, maxfun // 5 + 10)))

        x0 = np.zeros(r, dtype=np.float64)
        bounds = [(-3.0, 3.0)] * r

        try:
            res = minimize(
                obj.fgrad,
                x0,
                jac=True,
                method='L-BFGS-B',
                bounds=bounds,
                options={
                    'maxiter': maxiter,
                    'maxfun': maxfun,
                    'ftol': 1e-18,
                    'gtol': 1e-15,
                    'maxcor': 50,
                    'maxls': 30
                }
            )
            alpha = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64)
            if alpha.size == r and np.all(np.isfinite(alpha)):
                new_z = z0 + Q.dot(alpha)
                diff = new_z - z0
                diff = np.clip(diff, -3.0, 3.0)
                new_z = z0 + diff
                new_z = new_z - float(np.mean(new_z))
                new_a = _softmax(new_z)
                new_sc = _score(new_a)

                if new_sc < best_sc:
                    best_a = new_a.copy()
                    best_sc = new_sc

                if new_sc < sc + 1e-3 * max(1e-300, abs(sc)):
                    z0 = new_z
        except Exception:
            break

    try:
        a_z = _softmax(z0)
        sc_z = _score(a_z)
        if sc_z < best_sc:
            best_a = a_z.copy()
            best_sc = sc_z
    except Exception:
        pass

    return best_a, best_sc


def _weighted_polish(a_init, deadline, min_time_remaining, K_max=32):
    """
    Direct active-set polish in log-space.  For the current active peaks it
    solves for weights lambda in simplex and follows the softmax gradient of
    the corresponding weighted sum, with a short exact line search.
    """
    a = _normalize(a_init)
    n = int(a.size)
    z = _log_from_a(a)
    best_a = a.copy()
    best_sc = _score(a)

    phase_end = float(deadline) - float(min_time_remaining)
    if time.monotonic() >= phase_end - 5.0:
        return best_a, best_sc

    K = 8
    no_improve = 0
    resets = 0
    outer = 0

    while time.monotonic() < phase_end - 5.0:
        outer += 1
        if outer % 18 == 0 and K < K_max:
            K = min(K_max, K + 1)

        a = _softmax(z)
        c = _fft_convolve(a, a)
        sc = float(2.0 * n * c.max())
        if sc < best_sc:
            best_sc = sc
            best_a = a.copy()

        idx = _top_indices(c, K)
        if idx.size == 0:
            break

        G = _build_G(a, idx)
        c_active = np.asarray(c[idx], dtype=np.float64)

        improved = False

        # Primary direction: optimized active weights.
        try:
            lam = _solve_lambda(a, G, c_active, max_iter=22)
            h = G.dot(lam)
            W = float(np.dot(lam, c_active))
            p = h - W
            zdir = a * p
            if float(np.linalg.norm(zdir)) > 1e-18:
                scale = float(np.max(np.abs(zdir)))
                if scale > 1e-300:
                    zdir = zdir / scale
                    etas = (0.5, 0.25, 0.125, 0.0625, 0.03125)
                    for eta in etas:
                        z_try = z - eta * zdir
                        a_try = _softmax(z_try)
                        sc_try = _score(a_try)
                        if sc_try < sc - 1e-14:
                            z = z_try
                            a = a_try
                            sc = sc_try
                            improved = True
                            if sc < best_sc:
                                best_sc = sc
                                best_a = a.copy()
                            break
        except Exception:
            pass

        # Fallback direction: uniform active weights.
        if not improved:
            try:
                h = G.dot(np.ones(idx.size, dtype=np.float64) / max(1, idx.size))
                W = float(np.mean(c_active))
                p = h - W
                zdir = a * p
                if float(np.linalg.norm(zdir)) > 1e-18:
                    scale = float(np.max(np.abs(zdir)))
                    if scale > 1e-300:
                        zdir = zdir / scale
                        etas = (0.5, 0.25, 0.125, 0.0625, 0.03125)
                        for eta in etas:
                            z_try = z - eta * zdir
                            a_try = _softmax(z_try)
                            sc_try = _score(a_try)
                            if sc_try < sc - 1e-14:
                                z = z_try
                                a = a_try
                                sc = sc_try
                                improved = True
                                if sc < best_sc:
                                    best_sc = sc
                                    best_a = a.copy()
                                break
            except Exception:
                pass

        z = z - float(np.mean(z))

        if improved:
            no_improve = 0
        else:
            no_improve += 1
            if no_improve == 12 and K > 8:
                K = max(8, K // 2)
            if no_improve >= 45:
                z = _log_from_a(best_a)
                K = 8
                no_improve = 0
                resets += 1
                if resets > 6:
                    break

    return best_a, best_sc


def _simple_perturbations(a0, deadline, min_time_remaining):
    """
    Cheap deterministic multiplicative perturbations to escape mild symmetry
    traps before expensive optimization.
    """
    a0 = _normalize(a0)
    n = int(a0.size)
    best_a = a0.copy()
    best_sc = _score(a0)

    if n < 2 or time.monotonic() >= deadline - min_time_remaining - 2.0:
        return best_a, best_sc

    t = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
    lin = t - 0.5
    quad = (2.0 * t - 1.0) ** 2 - (1.0 / 3.0)
    binv = np.sign(lin) * np.abs(lin) ** 0.7
    shape3 = np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * 3.0 * t)
    shape7 = np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * 7.0 * t)
    u_shape = np.where(t < 0.5, t, 1.0 - t) - 0.5

    shapes = (lin, quad, binv, shape3, shape7, u_shape)
    strengths = (0.05, 0.15, 0.35)

    for strength in strengths:
        for sh in shapes:
            if time.monotonic() >= deadline - min_time_remaining - 2.0:
                return best_a, best_sc
            for sgn in (1, -1):
                factor = 1.0 + float(sgn) * float(strength) * sh
                factor = np.maximum(factor, 1e-3)
                a_try = _normalize(a0 * factor)
                sc_try = _score(a_try)
                if sc_try < best_sc:
                    best_sc = sc_try
                    best_a = a_try.copy()

    return best_a, best_sc


def _quick_resample_check(a0, deadline):
    """
    If a simple resampling happens to lower the score, use it.
    """
    a0 = _normalize(a0)
    n = int(a0.size)
    best_a = a0.copy()
    best_sc = _score(a0)

    cand = []
    if n * 2 <= 32768:
        cand.append(n * 2)
    if n * 3 <= 49152:
        cand.append(n * 3)
    if n // 2 >= 4096:
        cand.append(n // 2)
    if n != 16384:
        cand.append(16384)

    seen = set()
    for nn in cand:
        if nn in seen:
            continue
        seen.add(nn)
        if nn <= 1024 or nn > 49152 or nn == n:
            continue
        a_try = _resample(a0, nn)
        sc_try = _score(a_try)
        if sc_try < best_sc:
            best_sc = sc_try
            best_a = a_try.copy()
        if time.monotonic() >= deadline - 30.0:
            break

    return best_a, best_sc


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=1800):
    """
    Main entry point.  Returns a Python list of non-negative floats.
    """
    t_start = time.monotonic()
    deadline = t_start + max(1.0, float(budget_s) - 25.0)

    # Load parent if available.
    parent = None
    try:
        parent_raw = np.asarray(height_sequence_1, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        if parent_raw.size > 0:
            parent_raw = np.clip(parent_raw, 0.0, 1000.0)
            parent_raw = parent_raw[np.isfinite(parent_raw)]
            s = float(parent_raw.sum())
            if s > 0.01:
                parent = parent_raw / s
    except Exception:
        parent = None

    if parent is not None:
        best_seq = parent.copy()
        best_score = _score(best_seq)
        print(f"Parent: n={best_seq.size}, internal score={best_score:.10f}")
    else:
        best_seq = np.ones(16384, dtype=np.float64) / 16384.0
        best_score = _score(best_seq)
        print(f"No parent; uniform n=16384, internal score={best_score:.10f}")

    # --- Phase 0: cheap resampling / perturbation checks ---
    if time.monotonic() < deadline - 1600.0:
        best_seq, best_score = _quick_resample_check(best_seq, deadline)
        print(f"After resample check: score={best_score:.10f}, n={best_seq.size}")

    if time.monotonic() < deadline - 1580.0:
        best_seq, best_score = _simple_perturbations(best_seq, deadline, 1580.0)
        print(f"After simple perturbations: score={best_score:.10f}, n={best_seq.size}")

    # --- Phase 1: main full L-BFGS ---
    if time.monotonic() < deadline - 900.0:
        best_seq, best_score = _run_top_lbfgs(
            best_seq,
            deadline,
            min_time_remaining=900.0,
            max_K=512
        )
        print(f"After full L-BFGS: score={best_score:.10f}, n={best_seq.size}")

    # --- Phase 2: reduced-basis L-BFGS ---
    if time.monotonic() < deadline - 450.0:
        best_seq, best_score = _run_reduced_lbfgs(
            best_seq,
            deadline,
            min_time_remaining=450.0,
            K=48
        )
        print(f"After reduced L-BFGS: score={best_score:.10f}, n={best_seq.size}")

    # --- Phase 3: active-set weight polish ---
    if time.monotonic() < deadline - 220.0:
        best_seq, best_score = _weighted_polish(
            best_seq,
            deadline,
            min_time_remaining=220.0,
            K_max=32
        )
        print(f"After weighted polish: score={best_score:.10f}, n={best_seq.size}")

    # --- Phase 4: short multi-length exploration ---
    end_multi = deadline - 60.0
    if time.monotonic() < end_multi - 70.0:
        n_best = int(best_seq.size)
        cands = []
        if n_best * 2 <= 32768:
            cands.append(n_best * 2)
        if n_best // 2 >= 4096:
            cands.append(n_best // 2)
        if n_best != 16384:
            cands.append(16384)
        if n_best != 24576:
            cands.append(24576)

        seen = set()
        uniq = []
        for nn in cands:
            if nn in seen:
                continue
            seen.add(nn)
            if 4096 <= nn <= 32768 and nn != n_best:
                uniq.append(nn)

        if uniq:
            per = max(20.0, (end_multi - time.monotonic()) / max(1, len(uniq)))
            for nn in uniq[:3]:
                if time.monotonic() >= end_multi - 10.0:
                    break
                sub_dead = min(end_multi, time.monotonic() + per * 0.92)
                a_try = _resample(best_seq, nn)
                sc_try = _score(a_try)
                if sc_try < best_score:
                    best_seq = a_try.copy()
                    best_score = sc_try
                    print(f"  resample n={nn}: score={best_score:.10f}")
                try:
                    a_opt, sc_opt = _run_top_lbfgs(
                        a_try,
                        sub_dead,
                        min_time_remaining=0.0,
                        max_K=256
                    )
                    if sc_opt < best_score:
                        best_seq = a_opt.copy()
                        best_score = sc_opt
                        print(f"  multi-n n={nn}: score={best_score:.10f}")
                except Exception:
                    pass
            print(f"After multi-n: score={best_score:.10f}, n={best_seq.size}")

    # --- Phase 5: final ultra-fine full L-BFGS ---
    if time.monotonic() < deadline - 15.0:
        best_seq, best_score = _run_top_lbfgs(
            best_seq,
            deadline,
            min_time_remaining=15.0,
            max_K=256
        )
        print(f"After final L-BFGS: score={best_score:.10f}, n={best_seq.size}")

    # Final validation and output.
    final = np.asarray(best_seq, dtype=np.float64)
    final = np.clip(final, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = float(final.sum())
    if s < 0.01:
        final = np.ones(256, dtype=np.float64) / 256.0
    else:
        final = final / s

    final_sc = _score(final)
    print("=" * 70)
    print(f"FINAL internal score={final_sc:.10f}, n={final.size}, elapsed={time.monotonic()-t_start:.1f}s")

    try:
        ev = evaluate_sequence([float(x) for x in final])
        print(f"FINAL evaluate_sequence={ev:.10f}")
        if np.isfinite(ev) and ev < final_sc:
            final_sc = float(ev)
    except Exception as e:
        print(f"evaluate_sequence check failed: {e}")

    return [float(x) for x in final]
```
